# Review of the Blueprint–Lean alignment PRs #213–#223

Ten pull requests by littlebin530, opened on 5 November 2026, rewrite the AI-written layer 2 of chapters 5–14 so that it follows the Lean formalisation (slopmaster2 `08fcdf1cca`), and add Lean names and dependency arrows. They continue #210–#212 (chapters 2–4, see `LEAN-ALIGNMENT-PR-REVIEW.md`):

- #213, Chapter 5: Construction of f^!
- #214, Chapter 6: Constructibility of f^!
- #215, Chapter 7: The six operations on D^b_ctf
- #216, Chapter 8: Smooth base change
- #218, Chapter 9: Smooth purity
- #219, Chapter 10: Verdier and Poincaré duality
- #220, Chapter 11: The Lefschetz–Verdier formula
- #221, Chapter 12: The trace formula
- #222, Chapter 13: Weights
- #223, Chapter 14: Purity of real sheaves

The eleventh PR in the range, #217, is a blog change that is already merged (see the end).

This report describes every change and what it means for the blueprint. It covers 916 files. Each of the 640 rewritten or new texts was read against the readable tex, its layer-2 clause and the cited Lean. The PRs were compared with `main` at their base (`13eeb096a`) and at today's head (`7fd36e75c`, which includes #200 and #238).

## Summary

**The new mathematics is mostly sound, but the PRs should not be merged as they stand.**

What holds up:
- 290 of the 640 texts raise no issue, and 280 raise only minor ones. The reviewers found no wrong mathematics in the new proofs.
- The text of the human-written declarations is untouched, and each PR stays inside its own chapter.
- `blueprint check` passes on all ten, both alone and merged into `main` before #200.
- All 418 Lean names they add exist at `08fcdf1cca`, and none is newer than the pinned `f3cad3b`.

What stops a merge:
- **70 items drop what the declaration asserts.** Layer 2 is cut down to the narrower statement Lean proves, so it no longer states the human declaration: 19 in #215, 17 in #221, 9 in #218, and 25 in the other seven PRs. In about a dozen of these, Lean does prove the full statement, and the PR removed that link.
- **The edits would be overwritten.** As with #210–#212, only the generated markdown changes and `blueprint-layer-2.tex` is unchanged. The next `scripts/retex.py` run would restore the old texts and delete the two new clauses. Since #238 the layer-2 tex also has a proof for every clause, so the PRs' new clause proofs now compete with it.
- **Every PR conflicts with `main`.** With #200 merged there are 339 conflicting files in total. Both rewrite the arrows of the human declarations: these PRs remove 2,323 and add 686. #222 also brings back the error that #204 fixed (Zulip item #34).
- **New arrow cycles.** Merged into today's `main`, #213, #214, #215, #218 and #223 each add cycles. All ten together make a cycle cluster of 178 nodes, plus a two-node cycle.

"Relation to the tex" compares each new text with the readable tex and its layer-2 clause:
- *same*: reworded only.
- *clarified*: adds hypotheses or details implicit in the tex.
- *narrowed*: states less than the tex asserts.
- *broadened*: states more.
- *changed*: different content.
- *bypass*: says the tex assertion is "bypassed by the Lean route".

## Findings that need a decision

### Content dropped from layer 2 (70 major items)

In each item the clause used to restate (part of) the human declaration. It now states only what Lean proves, or a different auxiliary result, so no clause states the declaration's claim any more. The parent declaration's own text is unchanged.

Major items by PR: #213 2, #214 2, #215 19, #216 6, #218 9, #219 3, #220 4, #221 17, #222 5, #223 3. All 70 are in the tables under "Every changed text". They fall into a few patterns.

**Coefficients narrowed.** The tex works with any admissible ring, including finite rings like ℤ/ℓⁿ, which it says cannot be avoided. The clauses keep only the coefficients Lean covers (Q̄_ℓ, sometimes O_E or E).
- `proposition-six-operations-dctf--1`, `--3`, `--4`, `--5`, `--7` (#215): preservation of D_ctf and change of coefficients for Rf_\*, Rf_!, f^! and internal Hom. Only O_E→E, E→F, E→Q̄_ℓ and reduction mod ℓⁿ remain. Finite-ring maps A→B and O_E→O_F are dropped, so "for each map listed above" in the parents is no longer covered. There are 19 such items in #215.
- `lemma-change-coefficients-direct-image--2` (#215): cut from O_E→A to the residue field only. The previous link `isIso_derivedUpperStarExtendScalarsPushHom_integersModPow_app` (O_E→O_E/ℓⁿ) exists and was removed. The cut also contradicts `proposition-six-operations-dctf--3`.
- `theorem-trace-formula-frobenius-correspondence--1`, `lemma-additivity-open-subscheme-complement--1`, `--2`, `lemma-local-term-restriction-map--1`, … (#221): the chapter's finite-coefficient argument (finite A, any Frobenius correspondence ψ) is replaced by Q̄_ℓ Weil objects for a finite-order automorphism, mostly without a bypass notice. The human proofs rely on the finite-coefficient chain.
- `lemma-change-coefficients--1` (#216): only flat coefficient changes, and an abstract isomorphism of arrows instead of the commuting square. O_E→F_E is lost, although the proof of all-admissible--2 uses it.

**A named map replaced by "there is an isomorphism".**
- `theorem-smooth-purity-all-admissible-coefficients--1`, `--2`, `--9` (#218): the natural purity isomorphism for all K becomes "an isomorphism exists at the constant object", and base change holds only up to an unspecified τ. That is weaker even than Lean, which has the canonical `dualizingComplexTwistShiftIso`. The main theorem's clauses now say little more than "an isomorphism exists".
- `proposition-compatibility-purity-base-change--4` (#218): g′^\*γ_f(K) = γ_{f′}(g^\*K) becomes "some isomorphism τ of Tate twists", which is close to empty.
- `theorem-trace-isomorphism-top-degree--9`, `corollary-cup-product-pairing--1`, `corollary-degreewise-duality-over-fields--1` (#219): "tr_X is an isomorphism", the cup-product pairing and the β_K pairings become existence of some isomorphisms or pairings, explicitly not identified with the tex's. The human proof of the cup-product corollary needs that identification.
- `lemma-kunneth-one-identity-factor--1` (#216): now only "some isomorphism exists", not that the Künneth map κ_{M,Q} is one. The exact Lean statement `isIso_externalProductRightDerivedLowerStarMap_of_finite` was removed from the parent.

**A declaration's only clause now states a different result.**
- `lemma-smooth-morphisms-locally-constant-sheaves--1` (#214): now states an auxiliary "compact dual presentation" (`exists_smooth_compact_dual_model`, which Lean calls "auxiliary; not a blueprint node"). The nonempty S₀, the lisse direct image and the base change are gone. It also has the KaTeX errors listed below.
- `proposition-relative-dualizing-complex--1` (#218): now states Ls^\*ω_g ≅ Λ_S(d)[2d] for a closed section, not ω_f ≅ (ℤ/ℓⁿ)(d)[2d]. It is also retitled.
- `lemma-local-terms-point--1` (#220): now states two intermediate identities instead of LT_s(u) = tr(u|P), and e_u clashes with e_K.
- `proposition-lattices-representations-normal-scheme--1` (#222): coefficients go from E to Q̄_ℓ, and the lattice fibre functor and its independence of the lattice are dropped. What is left is phrased in Lean terms (Ω-points, path transport).
- `lemma-kunneth-field-coefficients--1` (#216): field-coefficient Künneth is replaced by a conditional dévissage criterion over finite rings.

**Called "bypassed by the Lean route", though Lean proves it.** In each case the PR removed the Lean name that states the full claim.
- `proposition-weights-under-usual-operations--17` (#222): Tate-twist weights. `WeilSheaf.IsPunctuallyPureOfWeight.tateTwist` and `IsMixedOfWeightsAtMost.tateTwist` exist. The `--7` and `--12` proofs also deny that `…dual` and `…upperStar` exist, but both do.
- `proposition-determinant-weights-tensor-operations--1`, `--2`, `lemma-tensor-exterior-powers-filtered-representations--1` (#223): equalities of determinant weights are weakened to ⊆, and the tensor filtration to a subquotient statement. The full statements are in Lean (`exists_isIrreducible_subquotient_tprod_detWeight_eq_add`, `CompositionSeries.exteriorPower_detWeight`, `hasGradedPieces_tprod`), in files the main proof does not import.
- `proposition-properties-trace--5` (#220): filtered additivity over a ring becomes a field-only long-exact-sequence statement. `DerivedCategory.trace_eq_trace_add_trace_of_shortExact` exists and was removed from the parent.
- `proposition-additivity-trace-classes--1`, `--2` (#220): (1) is replaced by additivity under four extra Hom-vanishing hypotheses. (2) is narrowed to graph correspondences, although the more general `traceClass_eq_add_of_isPullback` was the previous link.
- `lemma-extension-scalars-preserves-dctf--1` (#215): flat maps only, with finite Tor amplitude dropped as "bypassed". `constructibleFiniteTor_leftDerivedSheafExtendScalars` exists.
- `proposition-base-change-larger-algebraically-closed--1`, `theorem-smooth-base-change-all-admissible--2` (#216): vanishing of R^q h_\* for all q becomes the q = 1 step, and integral smooth base change becomes a reduction criterion. Lean has `isZero_higherDirectImage_constantSheaf` and `isIso_baseChangeMap_app_padicIntegers_of_smooth` (the latter was removed).

**A case the later text uses is dropped.**
- `proposition-comparison-etale-derived-category--4` (#213): the direct-image comparison for all of D(X_ét, A) is restricted to D⁺, and no node states the unbounded version any more. The tex relies on it later (around lines 9441–9478).
- `proposition-composition-open-restriction-compactifications-f--3` (#213): retitled "Identity isomorphism". It disclaims compatibility with composition, which follows for Q̄_ℓ from unit laws the PR itself links.
- `proposition-weights-under-usual-operations--12`, `theorem-weights-rf-shriek--2` (#222): restricted to immersions, and to constant coefficients, although each clause's own proof works in general.
- `corollary-functional-equation--7`, `--10` (#221): the global functional equation (determinant and sign forms) is replaced by linear-algebra steps, and no node states it any more.
- `proof/theorem-smooth-purity-all-admissible-coefficients--3`, `--5`, `--6` (#218): valid tex proofs are deleted. The new texts say they do not prove the clause, or that a comparison is missing that the tex gives right after `proposition-relative-dualizing-complex`.

### Conflicts

- **All ten PRs conflict with `main`.** With #200 merged, the conflicting files are 11, 24, 67, 30, 26, 43, 17, 55, 29 and 37 for #213–#223, 339 in all.
  - #200 set the arrows of the human declarations to the exact Lean facts.
  - These PRs rewrite the same `uses` lines by their own Lean reading: on human declarations they remove 2,323 arrows (mostly the rough #184 dependency sets) and add 686.
  - The PRs touch no file in common with each other.
- **`proposition-weights-under-usual-operations--9` (#222) brings back a fixed error.** The PR was written from the base before #204. Its "informal assertion" says again that the graded pieces of the dual are the duals of the given ones, which is Zulip #1441 item #34. Keep `main`'s wording when resolving. (#200 has since renumbered this clause as `--6`.)
- **`proof/theorem-verdier-biduality--1` (#219) conflicts with #203.** The PR's proof does not assert a twist: it allows an invertible tensor factor, which does not affect the test. It therefore covers the #203 fix, so taking the PR's text is safe.

### Arrows

- **New cycles.** `main` has none, and the rule is no circular arrows at any level. Proof arrows are counted for their statement. Merged into today's `main` (conflicts resolved to the PR's side):
  - #213: `proposition-compactness-proper-direct-image` → `proposition-forgetting-supports-composition` → `proposition-construction-independence-rf-shriek` → back (25-node cluster).
  - #214: `corollary-open-direct-image-over-field` → `theorem-generic-constructibility-base-change-rj` → `lemma-smooth-morphisms-locally-constant-sheaves` → `…smooth-morphism--6` → `definition-exceptional-base-change-map` → back (19 nodes).
  - #215: `definition-change-of-coefficients-maps` ⇄ `lemma-extension-scalars-preserves-dctf`, a two-node cycle made by two new statement arrows (19-node cluster). There is also a 9-node cluster through `theorem-verdier-biduality`.
  - #218: `proposition-sp-unique-adjoint` ⇄ `theorem-existence-exceptional-inverse-image` (58-node cluster).
  - #223: `proposition-h-1-curve-tate-module` ⇄ `proposition-kummer-duality-integral-tate-module`.
  - All ten merged: a 178-node cluster and the two-node cycle of #223.
  - On the PRs' own base (before #200) the cycles were in #214, #215 and #218.
- **Volume.** The PRs add 2,200 arrows and remove 2,355: 1,514 added on layer-2 clauses and their proofs, 686 on human declarations. Of the added arrows, 776 point to definitions, 476 to layer-2 clauses, and 268 to an item later in the order. Like #210–#212, they follow Lean dependencies.

### Lean names

- **Added names.** 617 tags, 418 distinct names. A source scan at `08fcdf1cca` (plus Mathlib, proetale and batteries) finds all but four.
  - Two of the four are Mathlib's `Adjunction.rightAdjointUniq` and `rightAdjointUniq_hom_app_counit`, which the scan missed.
  - The other two are auto-generated instance names (`…instIsFiniteHomSchemeSigmaPointOfFinite`, `…instIsClosedImmersionFixedSchemeιOfIsSeparated`) that match real anonymous instances; only `check --lean` can confirm them.
  - None is newer than the pinned `f3cad3b`, and there are no private names. As the descriptions say, no proof file has a `lean` field.
- **Removed names.** 166 are removed and 164 of them still exist. Only 40 reappear on another node, so about 124 Lean links are dropped outright. The reviewers found many that state the node exactly (see the "bypassed" items above).
- **OLD-PR-CHECK.md.** It lists 14 Lean links as missing in chapters 5, 6, 7, 9 and 10, left for these chapters' reviews. None was added, though all 14 names exist at `08fcdf1cca`. In #213, `lemma-proper-direct-image-unbounded-complexes--1` and `--2` are even set to `lean = []` as "bypassed". Yet `proper_indConstructible_unbounded` (and `_padicAlgCl`) and `proper_unbounded_window_iso` state them.

### How the PRs are made

- **Generated files edited, tex untouched.** The two new clauses, `lemma-inverse-image-tensor-product--6` (#213) and `theorem-grothendieck-lefschetz-trace-formula--9` (#221), have no `bp-r…` label in `blueprint-layer-2.tex`, and neither do the 207 new proof files. A retex run would undo all of it.
  - Reviewers noted that the readable tex is already étale where the layer-2 tex still says pro-étale (e.g. bp-r166–r178). So some "narrowings" to étale just catch up with the readable tex.
- **Descriptions.** GitHub access failed during the review, so only the descriptions of #213 and #221 were read; their structural claims hold. The commit messages give edit counts that don't match the files: #214 says 41 and changes 48, #216 says 48 and changes 43, #218 says 25 and changes 38.
- **Formulas.** #214 has four KaTeX errors, in `lemma-smooth-morphisms-locally-constant-sheaves--1` and its proof. `\Fell_Y`, `\Fell_S` and `\Fell_X` give a double subscript, since `\Fell` is `\mathbf F_\ell`. The other 12,473 formulas in changed files render.
- **Order.** In #223, two new proofs (`proof/corollary-determinant-weights--1`, `--2`) have no `order`. In #213, #214, #215, #220, #222 and #223, 36 new proofs reuse their statement's `order`, which `main` never does.

### Patterns rated minor

- **Bypass notices, used two ways.**
  - In most cases the tex statement is kept word for word behind "This statement is bypassed by the Lean route", with `lean = []`. That is harmless.
  - In the others the notice introduces a different, Lean-shaped statement. Those are the major items above.
- **`\Dbc` redefined.** #213, #215 and #216 redefine D^b_c in hidden text nodes "in the module-object sense used in Lean": presentation matrices need not come from Λ(\*). That differs from `definition-constructible-sheaves`, which the clauses still link.
- **Lean leaking into the text.** Words like "mainline", "Lean route", "supplied declaration index" and "human statement", condensed-ring and universe hypotheses, and proofs that are chains of backticked Lean names.
- **Notation.**
  - Letters are reused with new roles: γ, s, g, X, Y, h.
  - #214 switches to a Kummer-boundary sign convention (t_p = −Tr_p) but still writes Tr.
  - The new E_φ clashes with the field E.
- **Generalized to Lean's setting.** qcqs morphisms, any commutative ring, unbounded or bounded-below complexes, quasi-finite schemes. These are mostly fine.

## #217 (merged)

A blog change by mathhzstx, merged at 11:55. It moves the download link for the Lean 4 performance guide from the end of section 1 to the end of the lessons post, with one line saying the guide is a prompt for an agent. One file, +5/−6. Nothing to note.

## How this was checked

- **The change data.** Each PR's head was fetched, and every changed file was compared with the base `13eeb096a`: whether its text changed, which `lean` and `uses` entries were added or removed, and which other attributes changed.
- **The human-written text.** Checked mechanically: no declaration's text changed, and every file is inside its PR's chapter.
- **Tool checks.**
  - `blueprint check` (tool `17f7e02`, as pinned at the base) gives 0 errors and 0 warnings on each PR head. So does tool `6b7ab97` on each PR merged into `main` before #200 (`234732a0d`).
  - All formulas in changed files were rendered with KaTeX and the repo macros.
- **Cycles.** Found with a strongly-connected-component search on `uses`, with proof arrows counted for their statement. This was done at the PRs' base and merged into today's `main` (`7fd36e75c`).
- **Merges.** Tested with `git merge-tree` against `main` and against each other.
- **The texts.** Ten reviewers, one per PR, read each rewritten or new text against the readable tex, the layer-2 tex and the Lean source at `08fcdf1cca`. Each text was classed as same, clarified, narrowed, broadened, changed or bypass, with a severity, using the same rubric as for #210–#212. The "Lean has it" claims behind the major items were checked by hand against the source scan.
- **Lean names.** Checked by scanning declaration headers in the weil-conjectures sources at `08fcdf1cca` and at the pinned `f3cad3b`, plus the pinned Mathlib, proetale and batteries. This is not a Lean build.
